## Icon

An icon is a small visual element, like a symbol, that represents certain functions, features, or types of content within a user interface.

```typescript
import {Icon} from "@qualcomm-ui/react/icon"
```

### [Overview](https://react-next.qui.qualcomm.com/components/icon#overview)

QUI components use icons from [Lucide](https://lucide.dev/), an open-source fork of Feather Icons.

Many components like the [Button](/components/button) and [Icon Button](/components/icon-button) have built-in support for Lucide Icons.

### [Examples](https://react-next.qui.qualcomm.com/components/icon#examples)

#### [Showcase](https://react-next.qui.qualcomm.com/components/icon#showcase)

```tsx
import type {ReactNode} from "react"

import {ExternalLink, Eye, Search} from "lucide-react"

import {Icon} from "@qualcomm-ui/react/icon"

export function IconShowcaseDemo(): ReactNode {
  return (
    <div className="grid justify-center">
      <div className="q-foreground-1-primary flex gap-4">
        <Icon icon={ExternalLink} size="lg" />
        <Icon icon={Search} size="lg" />
        <Icon icon={Eye} size="lg" />
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

```

#### [Sizes](https://react-next.qui.qualcomm.com/components/icon#sizes)

Size can be customized using the [size](https://react-next.qui.qualcomm.com/components/icon#size) input. There are five available values: `xs`, `sm`, `md`, `lg`, `xl`. Should you need more control over the rendered size, supply a number instead which will be converted to pixels.

```tsx
import type {ReactNode} from "react"

import {ExternalLink} from "lucide-react"

import {Icon} from "@qualcomm-ui/react/icon"

export function IconSizesDemo(): ReactNode {
  return (
    <div className="q-foreground-1-primary grid justify-center gap-4">
      <div className="flex items-end justify-center gap-4">
        <Icon icon={ExternalLink} size="xs" />
        <Icon icon={ExternalLink} size="sm" />
        <Icon icon={ExternalLink} size="md" />
        <Icon icon={ExternalLink} size="lg" />
        <Icon icon={ExternalLink} size="xl" />
      </div>
      <div className="flex items-center justify-center gap-4">
        <Icon icon={ExternalLink} size={32} />
        <Icon icon={ExternalLink} size={42} />
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

```

### [API](https://react-next.qui.qualcomm.com/components/icon#api)

#### [\<Icon>](https://react-next.qui.qualcomm.com/components/icon#icon)

The properties of the `<svg>` element also apply.

```json
[
  {
    "name": "icon",
    "type": "LucideIcon",
    "description": "lucide-react icon.",
    "required": true
  },
  {
    "name": "size",
    "type": "'xs' | 'sm' | 'md' | 'lg' | 'xl' | string | number",
    "defaultValue": "'md'",
    "description": "The size of the icon. If supplied as a number, the value will be applied as px. Otherwise, the string value of the property will apply."
  }
]
```

## Icon Button

An icon button performs an action when clicked (like a button), and it visually represents the action it will perform (like an icon).

```tsx
import {IconButton} from "@qualcomm-ui/react/button"
```

### [Accessibility Guidelines](https://react-next.qui.qualcomm.com/components/icon-button#accessibility-guidelines)

Icon-only buttons rely on visual cues that screen readers cannot access. Use [aria-label](https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/Accessibility/ARIA/Reference/Attributes/aria-label) or [aria-labelledby](https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/Accessibility/ARIA/Reference/Attributes/aria-labelledby) to give every icon-only button an accessible name so its purpose is clear to assistive technologies.

### [Examples](https://react-next.qui.qualcomm.com/components/icon-button#examples)

#### [Variants](https://react-next.qui.qualcomm.com/components/icon-button#variants)

Buttons come in three variants: `fill`, `outline`, and `ghost`. Customize these with the [variant](https://react-next.qui.qualcomm.com/components/icon-button#variant) property.

```tsx
import type {ReactElement} from "react"

import {ExternalLink} from "lucide-react"

import {IconButton} from "@qualcomm-ui/react/button"

export function IconButtonVariantsDemo(): ReactElement {
  return (
    <div className="grid grid-cols-3 grid-rows-2 gap-x-8 gap-y-1">
      <div className="text-neutral-primary font-heading-xs">Fill</div>
      <div className="text-neutral-primary font-heading-xs">Outline</div>
      <div className="text-neutral-primary font-heading-xs">Ghost</div>

      <IconButton icon={ExternalLink} variant="fill" />
      <IconButton icon={ExternalLink} variant="outline" />
      <IconButton icon={ExternalLink} variant="ghost" />
    </div>
  )
}

```

#### [Emphasis](https://react-next.qui.qualcomm.com/components/icon-button#emphasis)

Control the visual emphasis with different colors: `neutral`, `primary`, and `danger`. Customize these with the [emphasis](https://react-next.qui.qualcomm.com/components/icon-button#emphasis) prop.

```tsx
import type {ReactElement} from "react"

import {ExternalLink} from "lucide-react"

import {IconButton} from "@qualcomm-ui/react/button"

export function IconButtonEmphasisDemo(): ReactElement {
  return (
    <div className="grid grid-cols-3 grid-rows-2 gap-x-8 gap-y-1">
      <div className="text-neutral-primary font-heading-xs">Neutral</div>
      <div className="text-neutral-primary font-heading-xs">Primary</div>
      <div className="text-neutral-primary font-heading-xs">Danger</div>

      <IconButton emphasis="neutral" icon={ExternalLink} variant="fill" />
      <IconButton emphasis="primary" icon={ExternalLink} variant="fill" />
      <IconButton emphasis="danger" icon={ExternalLink} variant="fill" />
    </div>
  )
}

```

#### [Sizes and Icons](https://react-next.qui.qualcomm.com/components/icon-button#sizes-and-icons)

Customize size using the [size](https://react-next.qui.qualcomm.com/components/icon-button#size) property. QUI buttons come with built-in support for [lucide-react](https://lucide.dev) icons.

```tsx
import type {ReactElement} from "react"

import {ExternalLink} from "lucide-react"

import {IconButton} from "@qualcomm-ui/react/button"

export function IconButtonSizesDemo(): ReactElement {
  return (
    <div className="grid justify-items-center gap-4">
      <IconButton
        emphasis="primary"
        icon={ExternalLink}
        size="sm"
        variant="fill"
      />
      <IconButton
        emphasis="primary"
        icon={ExternalLink}
        size="md"
        variant="fill"
      />
      <IconButton
        emphasis="primary"
        icon={ExternalLink}
        size="lg"
        variant="fill"
      />
    </div>
  )
}

```

#### [Compact Density](https://react-next.qui.qualcomm.com/components/icon-button#compact-density)

Use the [density](https://react-next.qui.qualcomm.com/components/icon-button#density) prop to reduce the padding and height of the button. The options are `default` and `compact`.

```tsx
import type {ReactElement} from "react"

import {ExternalLink} from "lucide-react"

import {IconButton} from "@qualcomm-ui/react/button"

export function IconButtonDensityDemo(): ReactElement {
  return (
    <div className="grid justify-items-center gap-4">
      <IconButton
        density="compact"
        emphasis="primary"
        icon={ExternalLink}
        size="sm"
        variant="fill"
      />
      <IconButton
        density="compact"
        emphasis="primary"
        icon={ExternalLink}
        size="md"
        variant="fill"
      />
      <IconButton
        density="compact"
        emphasis="primary"
        icon={ExternalLink}
        size="lg"
        variant="fill"
      />
    </div>
  )
}

```

#### [Variant Combinations](https://react-next.qui.qualcomm.com/components/icon-button#variant-combinations)

```tsx
import type {ReactElement} from "react"

import {Search} from "lucide-react"

import {IconButton} from "@qualcomm-ui/react/button"

export function IconButtonVariantCombinationsDemo(): ReactElement {
  return (
    <div className="grid grid-cols-3 grid-rows-4 gap-x-8 gap-y-5">
      <span className="text-neutral-primary font-heading-xs">Fill</span>
      <span className="text-neutral-primary font-heading-xs">Outline</span>
      <span className="text-neutral-primary font-heading-xs">Ghost</span>

      <IconButton emphasis="neutral" icon={Search} variant="fill" />
      <IconButton emphasis="neutral" icon={Search} variant="outline" />
      <IconButton emphasis="neutral" icon={Search} variant="ghost" />

      <IconButton emphasis="primary" icon={Search} variant="fill" />
      <IconButton emphasis="primary" icon={Search} variant="outline" />
      <IconButton emphasis="primary" icon={Search} variant="ghost" />

      <IconButton emphasis="danger" icon={Search} variant="fill" />
      <IconButton emphasis="danger" icon={Search} variant="outline" />
      <IconButton emphasis="danger" icon={Search} variant="ghost" />

      <IconButton disabled icon={Search} variant="fill" />
      <IconButton disabled icon={Search} variant="outline" />
      <IconButton disabled icon={Search} variant="ghost" />
    </div>
  )
}

```

#### [Contrast Colors](https://react-next.qui.qualcomm.com/components/icon-button#contrast-colors)

```tsx
import type {ReactElement} from "react"

import {ExternalLink} from "lucide-react"

import {IconButton} from "@qualcomm-ui/react/button"

export function IconButtonContrastDemo(): ReactElement {
  return (
    <div className="grid gap-4">
      <div className="bg-persistent-black flex gap-6 p-4">
        <IconButton
          emphasis="white-persistent"
          icon={ExternalLink}
          variant="fill"
        />
        <IconButton
          emphasis="white-persistent"
          icon={ExternalLink}
          variant="outline"
        />
        <IconButton
          emphasis="white-persistent"
          icon={ExternalLink}
          variant="ghost"
        />
      </div>

      <div className="bg-persistent-white flex gap-6 p-4">
        <IconButton
          emphasis="black-persistent"
          icon={ExternalLink}
          variant="fill"
        />
        <IconButton
          emphasis="black-persistent"
          icon={ExternalLink}
          variant="outline"
        />
        <IconButton
          emphasis="black-persistent"
          icon={ExternalLink}
          variant="ghost"
        />
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

```

### [API](https://react-next.qui.qualcomm.com/components/icon-button#api)

#### [\<IconButton>](https://react-next.qui.qualcomm.com/components/icon-button#icon-button)

```json
[
  {

    "type": "'default' | 'compact'",

  },
  {
    "name": "disabled",
    "type": "boolean",
```

## Inline Icon Button

```tsx
import {InlineIconButton} from "@qualcomm-ui/react/inline-icon-button"
```

### [Accessibility Guidelines](https://react-next.qui.qualcomm.com/components/inline-icon-button#accessibility-guidelines)

Icon-only buttons rely on visual cues that screen readers cannot access. Use [aria-label](https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/Accessibility/ARIA/Reference/Attributes/aria-label) or [aria-labelledby](https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/Accessibility/ARIA/Reference/Attributes/aria-labelledby) to give every icon-only button an accessible name so its purpose is clear to assistive technologies.

### [Examples](https://react-next.qui.qualcomm.com/components/inline-icon-button#examples)

#### [Showcase](https://react-next.qui.qualcomm.com/components/inline-icon-button#showcase)

```tsx
import type {ReactElement} from "react"

import {X} from "lucide-react"

import {InlineIconButton} from "@qualcomm-ui/react/inline-icon-button"

export function InlineIconButtonShowcaseDemo(): ReactElement {
  return (
    <InlineIconButton icon={X} />
  )
}

```

#### [Variants](https://react-next.qui.qualcomm.com/components/inline-icon-button#variants)

```tsx
import type {ReactElement} from "react"

import {X} from "lucide-react"

import {InlineIconButton} from "@qualcomm-ui/react/inline-icon-button"

export function InlineIconButtonVariantsDemo(): ReactElement {
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-3">
      <InlineIconButton icon={X} />
      <div className="bg-persistent-white p-2">
        <InlineIconButton emphasis="persistent-black" icon={X} />
      </div>
      <div className="bg-persistent-black p-2">
        <InlineIconButton emphasis="persistent-white" icon={X} />
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

```

### [API](https://react-next.qui.qualcomm.com/components/inline-icon-button#api)

#### [\<InlineIconButton>](https://react-next.qui.qualcomm.com/components/inline-icon-button#inline-icon-button)

```json
[
  {
    "name": "emphasis",
    "type": "'neutral' | 'persistent-white' | 'persistent-black'",
    "defaultValue": "'neutral'",
    "description": "The style variant of the button. Governs color. TODO: link to design system docs."
  },
  {
    "name": "icon",
    "type": "LucideIcon | ReactNode",
    "description": "Lucide icon to display inside the button.",
    "required": true
  },
  {
    "name": "render",
    "type": "ReactElement | (( props: object, ) => ReactElement)",
    "description": "Allows you to replace the component's HTML element with a different tag or component. [Learn more](https://react-next.qui.qualcomm.com/polymorphic-components)"
  },
  {
    "name": "size",
    "type": "'sm' | 'md' | 'lg'",
    "defaultValue": "'md'",
    "description": "The size of the button and its icon."
  },
  {
    "name": "variant",
    "type": "'fixed' | 'scale'",
    "defaultValue": "'fixed'",
    "description": "The style variant of the button's icon that controls its size\n\n**option:** `scale`: The icon size scales with the button's size\n\n**option:** `fixed`: The icon size is constant, regardless of the button's size"
  }
]
```

#### [Icon](https://react-next.qui.qualcomm.com/components/inline-icon-button#icon)

The inner icon element is rendered with the icon passed to the [icon](https://react-next.qui.qualcomm.com/components/inline-icon-button#icon) prop.

## Inline Notification

```tsx
import {InlineNotification} from "@qualcomm-ui/react/inline-notification"
```

### [Examples](https://react-next.qui.qualcomm.com/components/inline-notification#examples)

#### [Simple](https://react-next.qui.qualcomm.com/components/inline-notification#simple)

Basic inline notification using the simple API with heading and description.

```tsx
import type {ReactElement} from "react"

import {InlineNotification} from "@qualcomm-ui/react/inline-notification"

export function InlineNotificationSimpleDemo(): ReactElement {
  return (
    <InlineNotification
      className="w-96"
      description="Description"
      dismissable
      label="Label"
    />
  )
}

```

#### [Composite](https://react-next.qui.qualcomm.com/components/inline-notification#composite)

Use the composite API with individual components for more granular control.

```tsx
import type {ReactElement} from "react"

import {InlineNotification} from "@qualcomm-ui/react/inline-notification"

export function InlineNotificationCompositeDemo(): ReactElement {
  return (
    <InlineNotification.Root className="w-96">
      <InlineNotification.Icon />
      <InlineNotification.Label>Label</InlineNotification.Label>
      <InlineNotification.Description>
        Description
      </InlineNotification.Description>
    </InlineNotification.Root>
  )
}

```

#### [Emphasis (color)](https://react-next.qui.qualcomm.com/components/inline-notification#emphasis-color)

Control the visual emphasis with different colors: info, success, warning, danger, and neutral. Customize these with the [emphasis](https://react-next.qui.qualcomm.com/components/inline-notification#emphasis) prop.

```tsx
import type {ReactElement} from "react"

import {InlineNotification} from "@qualcomm-ui/react/inline-notification"

export function InlineNotificationEmphasisDemo(): ReactElement {
  return (
    <div className="grid w-96 gap-4">
      <InlineNotification emphasis="info" label="info" />
      <InlineNotification emphasis="success" label="success" />
      <InlineNotification emphasis="warning" label="warning" />
      <InlineNotification emphasis="danger" label="danger" />
      <InlineNotification emphasis="neutral" label="neutral" />
    </div>
  )
}

```

#### [Dismissable](https://react-next.qui.qualcomm.com/components/inline-notification#dismissable)

Add dismiss functionality to the simple notification using the [dismissable](https://react-next.qui.qualcomm.com/components/inline-notification#dismissable) prop. Use the `<InlineNotification.CloseButton />` component when using the composite API.

```tsx
import type {ReactElement} from "react"

import {InlineNotification} from "@qualcomm-ui/react/inline-notification"

export function InlineNotificationDismissableDemo(): ReactElement {
  return (
    <div className="flex w-96 flex-col gap-4">
      <InlineNotification dismissable label="Simple" />

      <InlineNotification.Root>
        <InlineNotification.Icon />
        <InlineNotification.Label>Composite</InlineNotification.Label>
        <InlineNotification.CloseButton />
      </InlineNotification.Root>
    </div>
  )
}

```

#### [Action](https://react-next.qui.qualcomm.com/components/inline-notification#action)

Include action links in notifications using the [action](https://react-next.qui.qualcomm.com/components/inline-notification#action) property or the `<InlineNotification.Action>` component via the composite API.

```tsx
import type {ReactElement} from "react"

import {InlineNotification} from "@qualcomm-ui/react/inline-notification"
import {Link} from "@qualcomm-ui/react/link"

export function InlineNotificationActionDemo(): ReactElement {
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
      <InlineNotification
        action={<Link>Text link</Link>}
        description="Description for action demo"
        label="Simple"
      />

      <InlineNotification.Root>
        <InlineNotification.Icon />
        <InlineNotification.Label>Composite</InlineNotification.Label>
        <InlineNotification.Description>
          Description for action demo
        </InlineNotification.Description>
        <InlineNotification.ActionContainer>
          <Link>Text link</Link>
        </InlineNotification.ActionContainer>
      </InlineNotification.Root>
    </div>
  )
}

```

#### [Layout](https://react-next.qui.qualcomm.com/components/inline-notification#layout)

Choose between inline and stacked layouts using the [layout](https://react-next.qui.qualcomm.com/components/inline-notification#layout) property. Stacked layout places the description and action below the content.

```tsx
import type {ReactElement} from "react"

import {InlineNotification} from "@qualcomm-ui/react/inline-notification"
import {Link} from "@qualcomm-ui/react/link"

export function InlineNotificationLayoutDemo(): ReactElement {
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
      <InlineNotification
        action={<Link size="sm">Text link</Link>}
        description="Description for horizontal orientation"
        dismissable
        label="Horizontal"
      />

      <InlineNotification
        action={<Link size="sm">Text link</Link>}
        description="Description for vertical orientation"
        dismissable
        label="Vertical"
        orientation="vertical"
      />
    </div>
  )
}

```

### [API](https://react-next.qui.qualcomm.com/components/inline-notification#api)

#### [\<InlineNotificaton>](https://react-next.qui.qualcomm.com/components/inline-notification#inline-notificaton)

The `InlineNotification` component supports every property from the [InlineNotification.Root](https://react-next.qui.qualcomm.com/components/inline-notification#inline-notification-root) component, plus the props listed below.

```json
[
  {
    "name": "action",
    "type": "ReactNode",
    "description": "The component used for the content of the action element."
  },
  {
    "name": "actionProps",
    "type": "InlineNotificationActionContainerProps",
    "description": "Props applied to the action container element."
  },
  {
    "name": "children",
    "type": "never",
    "description": "The simple InlineNotification doesn't support children."
  },
  {
    "name": "closeButtonProps",
    "type": "InlineNotificationCloseButtonProps",
    "description": "Props applied to the close button element."
  },
  {
    "name": "description",
    "type": "ReactNode",
    "description": "Optional description text for the notification."
  },
  {
    "name": "descriptionProps",
    "type": "InlineNotificationDescriptionProps",
    "description": "Props applied to the description element."
  },
  {
    "name": "dir",
    "type": "'ltr' | 'rtl'",
    "defaultValue": "'ltr'",
    "description": "The document's text/writing direction."
  },
  {
    "name": "dismissable",
    "type": "boolean",
    "defaultValue": "false",
    "description": "When `true`, renders a close button that dismisses the notification on click."
  },
  {
    "name": "emphasis",
    "type": "'info' | 'success' | 'warning' | 'danger' | 'neutral' | 'loading'",
    "defaultValue": "'info'",
    "description": "Governs the color of the notification and its icon."
  },
  {
    "name": "getRootNode",
    "type": "() => | Node | ShadowRoot | Document",
    "description": "A root node to correctly resolve document in custom environments. i.e., Iframes, Electron."
  },
  {
    "name": "icon",
    "type": "LucideIcon | ReactNode",
    "description": "Override the icon displayed in the notification. When this prop is omitted, the icon is determined by the emphasis prop."
  },
  {
    "name": "iconProps",
    "type": "InlineNotificationIconProps",
    "description": "Props applied to the icon element."
  },
  {
    "name": "id",
    "type": "string",
    "description": "[id attribute](https://www.w3schools.com/html/html_id.asp). If omitted, a unique identifier will be automatically generated for accessibility."
  },
  {
    "name": "label",
    "type": "ReactNode",
    "description": "Optional heading text for the notification."
  },
  {
    "name": "labelProps",
    "type": "InlineNotificationLabelProps",
    "description": "Props applied to the label element."
  },
  {
    "name": "onDismiss",
    "type": "() => void",
    "description": "Function invoked when the notification is dismissed"
  },
  {
    "name": "orientation",
    "type": "'horizontal' | 'vertical'",
    "defaultValue": "'horizontal'",
    "description": "The layout of the notification's elements.\n\n**option:** `'horizontal'`: The icon, title, description, link, and close button are displayed inline.\n\n**option:** `'vertical'`: The icon, title, and close button are placed on the first line, while the description, and link are stacked vertically and aligned with the title."
  },
  {
    "name": "render",
    "type": "ReactElement | (( props: object, ) => ReactElement)",
    "description": "Allows you to replace the component's HTML element with a different tag or component. [Learn more](https://react-next.qui.qualcomm.com/polymorphic-components)"
  },
  {
    "name": "role",
    "type": "'status' | 'alert'",
    "defaultValue": "'status'",
    "description": "The WAI-ARIA [role](https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/Accessibility/ARIA/Reference/Roles) of the notification.\n\n**option:** `'alert'`: Use this role when the notification is an alert that requires the user's immediate attention, like for errors or urgent information.\n\n**option:** `'status'`: Use this role when the notification is a status message that doesn't require the user's immediate attention, like for success or informational messages."
  }
]
```

## Link

```tsx
import {Link} from "@qualcomm-ui/react/link"
```

### [Examples](https://react-next.qui.qualcomm.com/components/link#examples)

#### [Emphasis (color)](https://react-next.qui.qualcomm.com/components/link#emphasis-color)

Links support different emphasis (colors) to match your design needs. Customize these with the [emphasis](https://react-next.qui.qualcomm.com/components/link#emphasis) property.

```tsx
import type {ReactElement} from "react"

import {Link} from "@qualcomm-ui/react/link"

export function LinkColorsDemo(): ReactElement {
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
      <Link>Default</Link>
      <Link emphasis="neutral">Neutral</Link>
    </div>
  )
}

```

#### [Icons](https://react-next.qui.qualcomm.com/components/link#icons)

Links can include icons to provide additional visual context. QUI links support [lucide-react](https://lucide.dev) icons.

```tsx
import type {ReactElement} from "react"

import {ChevronLeft, ChevronRight} from "lucide-react"

import {Link} from "@qualcomm-ui/react/link"

export function LinkIconsDemo(): ReactElement {
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
      <Link endIcon={ChevronRight}>Next Page</Link>
      <Link startIcon={ChevronLeft}>Go Back</Link>
    </div>
  )
}

```

#### [Sizes](https://react-next.qui.qualcomm.com/components/link#sizes)

Adjust the font and icon size of the link using the [size](https://react-next.qui.qualcomm.com/components/link#size) prop. The default size is `sm`.

```tsx
import type {ReactElement} from "react"

import {ExternalLink} from "lucide-react"

import {Link} from "@qualcomm-ui/react/link"

export function LinkSizesDemo(): ReactElement {
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col items-center gap-4">
      <Link endIcon={ExternalLink} size="xs">
        Link
      </Link>
      <Link endIcon={ExternalLink} size="sm">
        Link
      </Link>
      <Link endIcon={ExternalLink} size="md">
        Link
      </Link>
      <Link endIcon={ExternalLink} size="lg">
        Link
      </Link>
      <Link endIcon={ExternalLink} size="xl">
        Link
      </Link>
      <Link endIcon={ExternalLink} size="xxl">
        Link
      </Link>
    </div>
  )
}

```

#### [Disabled](https://react-next.qui.qualcomm.com/components/link#disabled)

When [disabled](https://react-next.qui.qualcomm.com/components/link#disabled) is true, the link becomes non-interactive and is rendered with reduced opacity to indicate its unavailable state.

```tsx
import type {ReactElement} from "react"

import {ExternalLink} from "lucide-react"

import {Link} from "@qualcomm-ui/react/link"

export function LinkDisabledDemo(): ReactElement {
  return (
    <Link disabled endIcon={ExternalLink}>
      Disabled
    </Link>
  )
}

```

#### [Render Prop](https://react-next.qui.qualcomm.com/components/link#render-prop)

Use the [render](https://react-next.qui.qualcomm.com/components/link#render) prop to render the link component as a different element, such as a React Router Link for client-side navigation. Refer to our [Polymorphic Components](/polymorphic-components) guide to learn more.

```tsx
import type {ReactElement} from "react"

import {Link as ReactRouterLink} from "react-router"

import {Link} from "@qualcomm-ui/react/link"

export function LinkRenderPropDemo(): ReactElement {
  return (
    <Link render={<ReactRouterLink to="/components/link#as-prop" />}>Link</Link>
  )
}

```

## Menu

```tsx
import {Menu} from "@qualcomm-ui/react/menu"
```

### [Examples](https://react-next.qui.qualcomm.com/components/menu#examples)

#### [Item Customization](https://react-next.qui.qualcomm.com/components/menu#item-customization)

Compose the menu to include icons and commands.

```tsx
import type {ReactElement} from "react"

import {Command, File, FileText, FolderOpen, ImageDown} from "lucide-react"

import {Icon} from "@qualcomm-ui/react/icon"
import {Menu} from "@qualcomm-ui/react/menu"
import {Portal} from "@qualcomm-ui/react-core/portal"

export function MenuItemCustomizationDemo(): ReactElement {
  return (
    <Menu.Root>
      <Menu.Trigger>
        <Menu.Button emphasis="primary">Show Menu</Menu.Button>
      </Menu.Trigger>
      <Portal>
        <Menu.Positioner>
          <Menu.Content>
            <Menu.Item value="new-text-file">
              <Menu.ItemStartIcon icon={FileText} />
              New Text File
              <Menu.ItemCommand>
                <Icon icon={Command} size="xs" />E
              </Menu.ItemCommand>
            </Menu.Item>
            <Menu.Item value="new-file">
              <Menu.ItemStartIcon icon={File} />
              New File...
              <Menu.ItemCommand>
                <Icon icon={Command} size="xs" />N
              </Menu.ItemCommand>
            </Menu.Item>
            <Menu.Item value="open-file">
              <Menu.ItemStartIcon icon={FolderOpen} />
              Open File...
              <Menu.ItemCommand>
                <Icon icon={Command} size="xs" />O
              </Menu.ItemCommand>
              <Menu.ItemDescription>Optional Description</Menu.ItemDescription>
            </Menu.Item>
            <Menu.Item value="export">
              <Menu.ItemStartIcon icon={ImageDown} />
              Export
              <Menu.ItemCommand>
                <Icon icon={Command} size="xs" />S
              </Menu.ItemCommand>
            </Menu.Item>
          </Menu.Content>
        </Menu.Positioner>
      </Portal>
    </Menu.Root>
  )
}

```

#### [Context Menu](https://react-next.qui.qualcomm.com/components/menu#context-menu)

Demonstrates how to trigger a menu on right-click or other context events.

```tsx
import type {ReactElement} from "react"

import {Menu} from "@qualcomm-ui/react/menu"
import {Portal} from "@qualcomm-ui/react-core/portal"

export function MenuContextMenuDemo(): ReactElement {
  return (
    <Menu.Root>
      <Menu.ContextTrigger className="flex h-48 w-full items-center justify-center border border-dashed">
        Right click here
      </Menu.ContextTrigger>
      <Portal>
        <Menu.Positioner>
          <Menu.Content>
            <Menu.ItemGroup>
              <Menu.Item value="new-txt">New Text File</Menu.Item>
              <Menu.Item value="new-file">New File...</Menu.Item>
              <Menu.Item value="open-file">Open File...</Menu.Item>
              <Menu.Item value="export">Export</Menu.Item>
            </Menu.ItemGroup>
          </Menu.Content>
        </Menu.Positioner>
      </Portal>
    </Menu.Root>
  )
}

```

#### [Nested Menus](https://react-next.qui.qualcomm.com/components/menu#nested-menus)

Compose nested menus by nesting `<Menu.Root>` components inside each other.

```tsx
import type {ReactElement} from "react"

import {Menu} from "@qualcomm-ui/react/menu"
import {Portal} from "@qualcomm-ui/react-core/portal"

export function MenuNestedDemo(): ReactElement {
  return (
    <Menu.Root>
      <Menu.Trigger>
        <Menu.Button emphasis="primary">Show Menu</Menu.Button>
      </Menu.Trigger>
      <Portal>
        <Menu.Positioner>
          <Menu.Content>
            <Menu.Item value="new-txt">New Text File</Menu.Item>
            <Menu.Item value="new-file">New File...</Menu.Item>
            <Menu.Root positioning={{gutter: 2, placement: "right-start"}}>
              <Menu.TriggerItem value="open-recent">
                Open Recent
              </Menu.TriggerItem>
              <Portal>
                <Menu.Positioner>
                  <Menu.Content>
                    <Menu.Item value="file-1">File 1</Menu.Item>
                    <Menu.Item value="file-2">File 2</Menu.Item>
                    <Menu.Item value="file-3">File 3</Menu.Item>
                  </Menu.Content>
                </Menu.Positioner>
              </Portal>
            </Menu.Root>
          </Menu.Content>
        </Menu.Positioner>
      </Portal>
    </Menu.Root>
  )
}

```

#### [Links](https://react-next.qui.qualcomm.com/components/menu#links)

Menu items can be links using [polymorphic composition](/polymorphic-components). Use the [render](https://react-next.qui.qualcomm.com/components/menu#render) prop on the `<Menu.Item>` to specify the element type.

```tsx
import type {ReactElement} from "react"

import {ExternalLink} from "lucide-react"

import {Menu} from "@qualcomm-ui/react/menu"
import {Portal} from "@qualcomm-ui/react-core/portal"

export function MenuLinksDemo(): ReactElement {
  return (
    <Menu.Root>
      <Menu.Trigger>
        <Menu.Button emphasis="primary">Show Menu</Menu.Button>
      </Menu.Trigger>
      <Portal>
        <Menu.Positioner>
          <Menu.Content>
            <Menu.Item
              render={
                <a href="https://react.dev" rel="noreferrer" target="_blank" />
              }
              value="react"
            >
              <Menu.ItemStartIcon icon={ExternalLink} />
              React
            </Menu.Item>
            <Menu.Item
              render={
                <a
                  href="https://angular.dev"
                  rel="noreferrer"
                  target="_blank"
                />
              }
              value="angular"
            >
              <Menu.ItemStartIcon icon={ExternalLink} />
              Angular
            </Menu.Item>
          </Menu.Content>
        </Menu.Positioner>
      </Portal>
    </Menu.Root>
  )
}

```

#### [Groups](https://react-next.qui.qualcomm.com/components/menu#groups)

Use the `<Menu.ItemGroup>` component to group menu items.

#### [Item Types](https://react-next.qui.qualcomm.com/components/menu#item-types)

There are three item types, each corresponding to one of the three types of aria menu item roles:

* `<Menu.Item>` — `menuitem`
* `<Menu.RadioItem>` — `menuitemradio`
* `<Menu.CheckboxItem>` — `menuitemcheckbox`

##### [Checkbox Items](https://react-next.qui.qualcomm.com/components/menu#checkbox-items)

There are two appearance options:

* For a checkbox appearance, render the `<Menu.CheckboxItemControl>` element as a child of the item.
* Render the `<Menu.ItemIndicator />` element for a simple selection indicator.

```tsx
import type {ReactElement} from "react"

import {Menu} from "@qualcomm-ui/react/menu"
import {Portal} from "@qualcomm-ui/react-core/portal"

export function MenuCheckboxItemsDemo(): ReactElement {
  return (
    <Menu.Root>
      <Menu.Trigger>
        <Menu.Button emphasis="primary">Show Menu</Menu.Button>
      </Menu.Trigger>
      <Portal>
        <Menu.Positioner>
          <Menu.Content>
            <Menu.ItemGroup>
              <Menu.ItemGroupLabel>Choose an option</Menu.ItemGroupLabel>
              <Menu.CheckboxItem value="item-1-1">
                <Menu.CheckboxItemControl />
                <Menu.ItemLabel>Option 1</Menu.ItemLabel>
              </Menu.CheckboxItem>
              <Menu.CheckboxItem value="item-1-2">
                <Menu.CheckboxItemControl />
                <Menu.ItemLabel>Option 2</Menu.ItemLabel>
              </Menu.CheckboxItem>
            </Menu.ItemGroup>

            <Menu.ItemGroup>
              <Menu.ItemGroupLabel>Choose an option</Menu.ItemGroupLabel>
              <Menu.CheckboxItem value="item-2-1">
                <Menu.ItemLabel>Option 1</Menu.ItemLabel>
                <Menu.ItemIndicator />
              </Menu.CheckboxItem>
              <Menu.CheckboxItem value="item-2-2">
                <Menu.ItemLabel>Option 2</Menu.ItemLabel>
                <Menu.ItemIndicator />
              </Menu.CheckboxItem>
            </Menu.ItemGroup>
          </Menu.Content>
        </Menu.Positioner>
      </Portal>
    </Menu.Root>
  )
}

```

##### [Checkbox Selection State](https://react-next.qui.qualcomm.com/components/menu#checkbox-selection-state)

Illustrates how to manage and reflect the checked state of checkbox menu items.

```tsx
import {type ReactElement, useState} from "react"

import {Menu} from "@qualcomm-ui/react/menu"
import {Portal} from "@qualcomm-ui/react-core/portal"

const items: {label: string; value: string}[] = [
  {label: "Option 1", value: "item-1"},
  {label: "Option 2", value: "item-2"},
  {label: "Option 3", value: "item-3"},
]

export function MenuCheckboxSelectionStateDemo(): ReactElement {
  const [checkboxState, setCheckboxState] = useState<Record<string, boolean>>({
    [items[0].value]: true,
  })

  const onChange = (value: string, checked: boolean) => {
    setCheckboxState((prevState) => ({...prevState, [value]: checked}))
  }

  return (
    <Menu.Root>
      <Menu.Trigger>
        <Menu.Button emphasis="primary">Show Menu</Menu.Button>
      </Menu.Trigger>
      <Portal>
        <Menu.Positioner>
          <Menu.Content>
            <Menu.ItemGroup>
              <Menu.ItemGroupLabel>Choose an option</Menu.ItemGroupLabel>
              {items.map(({label, value}) => (
                <Menu.CheckboxItem
                  key={value}
                  checked={checkboxState[value] || false}
                  onCheckedChange={(checked) => onChange(value, checked)}
                  value={value}
                >
                  <Menu.ItemLabel>{label}</Menu.ItemLabel>
                  <Menu.ItemIndicator />
                </Menu.CheckboxItem>
              ))}
            </Menu.ItemGroup>
          </Menu.Content>
        </Menu.Positioner>
      </Portal>
    </Menu.Root>
  )
}

```

##### [Radio Group](https://react-next.qui.qualcomm.com/components/menu#radio-group)

Demonstrates how to group radio menu items so only one can be selected at a time.

```tsx
import type {ReactElement} from "react"

import {Menu} from "@qualcomm-ui/react/menu"
import {Portal} from "@qualcomm-ui/react-core/portal"

export function MenuRadioGroupDemo(): ReactElement {
  return (
    <Menu.Root>
      <Menu.Trigger>
        <Menu.Button emphasis="primary">Show Menu</Menu.Button>
      </Menu.Trigger>
      <Portal>
        <Menu.Positioner>
          <Menu.Content>
            <Menu.RadioItemGroup>
              <Menu.ItemGroupLabel>Choose an option</Menu.ItemGroupLabel>
              <Menu.RadioItem value="one">
                <Menu.RadioItemControl />
                <Menu.ItemLabel>Option 1</Menu.ItemLabel>
              </Menu.RadioItem>
              <Menu.RadioItem value="two">
                <Menu.RadioItemControl />
                <Menu.ItemLabel>Option 2</Menu.ItemLabel>
              </Menu.RadioItem>
              <Menu.RadioItem value="three">
                <Menu.RadioItemControl />
                <Menu.ItemLabel>Option 3</Menu.ItemLabel>
              </Menu.RadioItem>
            </Menu.RadioItemGroup>
          </Menu.Content>
        </Menu.Positioner>
      </Portal>
    </Menu.Root>
  )
}

```

#### [Controlled State](https://react-next.qui.qualcomm.com/components/menu#controlled-state)

The menu's visibility can be controlled via the `open`, `onOpenChange`, and `defaultOpen` props.

* Use `open` to explicitly set whether the menu is open (controlled mode).
* Use `onOpenChange` to be notified when the menu requests to open or close.
* Use `defaultOpen` to set the initial open state when you don't need to control it yourself (uncontrolled mode).

In controlled mode, the `open` prop should be updated in response to `onOpenChange` events to reflect the desired visibility.

```tsx
import {type ReactElement, useState} from "react"

import {Menu} from "@qualcomm-ui/react/menu"
import {Portal} from "@qualcomm-ui/react-core/portal"

export function MenuControlledStateDemo(): ReactElement {
  const [open, setOpen] = useState<boolean>(false)

  return (
    <Menu.Root onOpenChange={(nextState) => setOpen(nextState)} open={open}>
      <Menu.Trigger>
        <Menu.Button emphasis="primary">Show Menu</Menu.Button>
      </Menu.Trigger>
      <Portal>
        <Menu.Positioner>
          <Menu.Content>
            <Menu.Item value="new-txt">New Text File</Menu.Item>
            <Menu.Item value="new-file">New File...</Menu.Item>
            <Menu.Item value="new-win">New Window</Menu.Item>
            <Menu.Item value="open-file">Open File...</Menu.Item>
            <Menu.Item value="export">Export</Menu.Item>
          </Menu.Content>
        </Menu.Positioner>
      </Portal>
    </Menu.Root>
  )
}

```

#### [Placement](https://react-next.qui.qualcomm.com/components/menu#placement)

Configure the menu's placement using the [positioning.placement](https://react-next.qui.qualcomm.com/components/menu#positioning) prop.

```tsx
import type {ReactElement} from "react"

import {Menu} from "@qualcomm-ui/react/menu"
import {Portal} from "@qualcomm-ui/react-core/portal"

export function MenuPlacementDemo(): ReactElement {
  return (
    <Menu.Root positioning={{placement: "right-start"}}>
      <Menu.Trigger>
        <Menu.Button emphasis="primary">Show Menu</Menu.Button>
      </Menu.Trigger>
      <Portal>
        <Menu.Positioner>
          <Menu.Content>
            <Menu.Item value="new-txt">New Text File</Menu.Item>
            <Menu.Item value="new-file">New File...</Menu.Item>
            <Menu.Item value="new-win">New Window</Menu.Item>
            <Menu.Item value="open-file">Open File...</Menu.Item>
            <Menu.Item value="export">Export</Menu.Item>
          </Menu.Content>
        </Menu.Positioner>
      </Portal>
    </Menu.Root>
  )
}

```

#### [Sizes](https://react-next.qui.qualcomm.com/components/menu#sizes)

Use the [size](https://react-next.qui.qualcomm.com/components/menu#size) prop to control the size of the menu. The default size is `md`. The demo features the small (`sm`) size.

```tsx
import type {ReactElement} from "react"

import {LogOut, Settings, User} from "lucide-react"

import {Menu} from "@qualcomm-ui/react/menu"
import {Portal} from "@qualcomm-ui/react-core/portal"

export function MenuSizesDemo(): ReactElement {
  return (
    <Menu.Root size="sm">
      <Menu.Trigger>
        <Menu.Button emphasis="primary">Show Menu</Menu.Button>
      </Menu.Trigger>
      <Portal>
        <Menu.Positioner>
          <Menu.Content>
            <Menu.Item value="account">
              <Menu.ItemStartIcon icon={User} />
              Account
            </Menu.Item>
            <Menu.Item value="settings">
              <Menu.ItemStartIcon icon={Settings} />
              Settings
            </Menu.Item>
            <Menu.Item value="logout">
              <Menu.ItemStartIcon icon={LogOut} />
              Logout
            </Menu.Item>
          </Menu.Content>
        </Menu.Positioner>
      </Portal>
    </Menu.Root>
  )
}

```

#### [Anchor Point](https://react-next.qui.qualcomm.com/components/menu#anchor-point)

Use the [positioning.anchorPoint](https://react-next.qui.qualcomm.com/components/menu#positioning) prop to control the anchor point of the menu.

```tsx
import {type ReactElement, useRef} from "react"

import {Menu} from "@qualcomm-ui/react/menu"
import {Portal} from "@qualcomm-ui/react-core/portal"

export function MenuAnchorPointDemo(): ReactElement {
  const ref = useRef<HTMLDivElement | null>(null)
  const getAnchorRect = () => ref.current!.getBoundingClientRect()

  return (
    <Menu.Root positioning={{getAnchorRect}}>
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
        <Menu.Trigger>
          <Menu.Button emphasis="primary">Show Menu</Menu.Button>
        </Menu.Trigger>
        <div
          ref={ref}
          className="font-body-lg text-neutral-primary bg-neutral-04 border-neutral-01 rounded-md border p-4"
        >
          Anchor
        </div>
      </div>
      <Portal>
        <Menu.Positioner>
          <Menu.Content>
            <Menu.Item value="new-txt">New Text File</Menu.Item>
            <Menu.Item value="new-file">New File...</Menu.Item>
            <Menu.Item value="new-win">New Window</Menu.Item>
            <Menu.Item value="open-file">Open File...</Menu.Item>
            <Menu.Item value="export">Export</Menu.Item>
          </Menu.Content>
        </Menu.Positioner>
      </Portal>
    </Menu.Root>
  )
}

```

#### [Avatar](https://react-next.qui.qualcomm.com/components/menu#avatar)

Here's an example that composes the `Menu` with the [Avatar](/components/avatar) component to display a user account menu.

```tsx
import type {ReactElement} from "react"

import {LogOut, Settings, User} from "lucide-react"

import {Avatar} from "@qualcomm-ui/react/avatar"
import {Menu} from "@qualcomm-ui/react/menu"
import {Portal} from "@qualcomm-ui/react-core/portal"

export function MenuAvatarDemo(): ReactElement {
  return (
    <Menu.Root positioning={{placement: "right-start"}}>
      <Menu.Trigger>
        <Avatar.Root render={<button />} status="active">
          <Avatar.Image alt="John Doe" src="/images/avatar-man.png" />
          <Avatar.Content>JD</Avatar.Content>
          <Avatar.Status />
        </Avatar.Root>
      </Menu.Trigger>
      <Portal>
        <Menu.Positioner>
          <Menu.Content>
            <Menu.Item value="account">
              <Menu.ItemStartIcon icon={User} />
              Account
            </Menu.Item>
            <Menu.Item value="settings">
              <Menu.ItemStartIcon icon={Settings} />
              Settings
            </Menu.Item>
            <Menu.Item value="logout">
              <Menu.ItemStartIcon icon={LogOut} />
              Logout
            </Menu.Item>
          </Menu.Content>
        </Menu.Positioner>
      </Portal>
    </Menu.Root>
  )
}

```

#### [Within Dialog](https://react-next.qui.qualcomm.com/components/menu#within-dialog)

When using the `Menu` inside a `Dialog`, don't render the `<Menu.Positioner>` within a `<Portal>` element.

```tsx
import type {ReactElement} from "react"

import {Button} from "@qualcomm-ui/react/button"
import {Dialog} from "@qualcomm-ui/react/dialog"
import {Menu} from "@qualcomm-ui/react/menu"
import {Portal} from "@qualcomm-ui/react-core/portal"

export function MenuWithinDialogDemo(): ReactElement {
  return (
    <Dialog.Root>
      <Dialog.Trigger>
        <Button emphasis="primary" variant="fill">
          Show Dialog
        </Button>
      </Dialog.Trigger>

      <Portal>
        <Dialog.Backdrop />
        <Dialog.Positioner>
          <Dialog.Content className="w-72">
            <Dialog.Body>
              <Dialog.Heading>Dialog Title</Dialog.Heading>
              <Dialog.CloseButton />

              <Menu.Root>
                <Menu.Trigger>
                  <Menu.Button className="place-self-start" emphasis="primary">
                    Show Menu
                  </Menu.Button>
                </Menu.Trigger>
                <Menu.Positioner>
                  <Menu.Content>
                    <Menu.Item value="new-txt">New Text File</Menu.Item>
                    <Menu.Item value="new-file">New File...</Menu.Item>
                    <Menu.Item value="new-win">New Window</Menu.Item>
                    <Menu.Item value="open-file">Open File...</Menu.Item>
                    <Menu.Item value="export">Export</Menu.Item>
                  </Menu.Content>
                </Menu.Positioner>
              </Menu.Root>
            </Dialog.Body>
          </Dialog.Content>
        </Dialog.Positioner>
      </Portal>
    </Dialog.Root>
  )
}

```

#### [Hide When Detached](https://react-next.qui.qualcomm.com/components/menu#hide-when-detached)

Use the [positioning.hideWhenDetached](https://react-next.qui.qualcomm.com/components/menu#positioning) prop to hide the menu when it's detached from the trigger.

```tsx
import type {ReactElement} from "react"

import {Button} from "@qualcomm-ui/react/button"
import {Menu} from "@qualcomm-ui/react/menu"
import {Portal} from "@qualcomm-ui/react-core/portal"

export function MenuHideWhenDetachedDemo(): ReactElement {
  return (
    <Menu.Root open positioning={{hideWhenDetached: true}}>
      <div className="border-neutral-03 flex max-w-72 gap-2 overflow-x-scroll rounded-md border p-4">
        {[...Array(6).keys()].map((x) => (
          <div
            key={x}
            className="font-body-md text-neutral-primary border-neutral-01 bg-neutral-04 rounded-md border p-3 whitespace-nowrap"
          >
            Item {x}
          </div>
        ))}
        <Menu.Trigger>
          <Button className="whitespace-nowrap" emphasis="primary">
            Show Menu
          </Button>
        </Menu.Trigger>
      </div>

      <Portal>
        <Menu.Positioner>
          <Menu.Content>
            <Menu.Item value="new-txt">New Text File</Menu.Item>
            <Menu.Item value="new-file">New File...</Menu.Item>
            <Menu.Item value="new-win">New Window</Menu.Item>
            <Menu.Item value="open-file">Open File...</Menu.Item>
            <Menu.Item value="export">Export</Menu.Item>
          </Menu.Content>
        </Menu.Positioner>
      </Portal>
    </Menu.Root>
  )
}

```

### [API](https://react-next.qui.qualcomm.com/components/menu#api)

#### [\<Menu.Root>](https://react-next.qui.qualcomm.com/components/menu#menu-root)

```json
[
  {
    "name": "anchorPoint",
    "type": "{ x: number y: number }",
    "description": "The positioning point for the menu. Can be set by the context menu trigger or the button trigger."
  },
  {
    "name": "children",
    "type": "ReactNode",
    "description": "React [children](https://react.dev/learn/passing-props-to-a-component#passing-jsx-as-children) prop.",
    "required": true
  },
  {
    "name": "closeOnSelect",
    "type": "boolean",
    "defaultValue": "true",
    "description": "Whether to close the menu when an option is selected"
  },
  {
    "name": "composite",
    "type": "boolean",
    "defaultValue": "true",
    "description": "Whether the menu is composed with other composite widgets like a combobox or tabs"
  },
  {
    "name": "defaultHighlightedValue",
    "type": "string",
    "description": "The initial highlighted value of the menu item when rendered. Use when you don't need to control the highlighted value of the menu item."
  },
  {
    "name": "defaultOpen",
    "type": "boolean",
    "description": "The initial open state of the menu when rendered. Use when you don't need to control the open state of the menu."
  },
  {
    "name": "dir",
    "type": "'ltr' | 'rtl'",
    "defaultValue": "'ltr'",
    "description": "The document's text/writing direction."
  },
  {
    "name": "getRootNode",
    "type": "() => | Node | ShadowRoot | Document",
    "description": "A root node to correctly resolve document in custom environments. i.e., Iframes, Electron."
  },
  {
    "name": "highlightedValue",
    "type": "string",
    "description": "The controlled highlighted value of the menu item."
  },
  {
    "name": "id",
    "type": "string",
    "description": "[id attribute](https://www.w3schools.com/html/html_id.asp). If omitted, a unique identifier will be automatically generated for accessibility."
  },
  {
    "name": "immediate",
    "type": "boolean",
    "description": "Whether to synchronize the present change immediately or defer it to the next frame"
  },
  {
    "name": "lazyMount",
    "type": "boolean",
    "defaultValue": "false",
    "description": "When true, the component will not be rendered in the DOM until it becomes visible or active."
  },
  {
    "name": "loopFocus",
    "type": "boolean",
    "defaultValue": "false",
    "description": "Whether to loop the keyboard navigation."
  },

    "name": "navigate",

  {
    "name": "onSelect",
```

#### [PositioningOptions](https://react-next.qui.qualcomm.com/components/menu#positioning-options)

The [positioning](https://react-next.qui.qualcomm.com/components/menu#positioning) prop accepts an object with the following shape:

```json
[
  {
    "name": "arrowPadding",
    "type": "number",
    "defaultValue": "4",
    "description": "The minimum padding between the arrow and the floating element's corner."
  },
  {
    "name": "boundary",
    "type": "() => | 'clippingAncestors' | Element | Array<Element> | { height: number width: number x: number y: number }",
    "description": "The overflow boundary of the reference element"
  },
  {
    "name": "fitViewport",
    "type": "boolean",
    "description": "Whether the popover should fit the viewport."
  },
  {
    "name": "flip",
    "type": "boolean | Array< | 'bottom' | 'bottom-end' | 'bottom-start' | 'left' | 'left-end' | 'left-start' | 'right' | 'right-end' | 'right-start' | 'top' | 'top-end' | 'top-start' >",
    "defaultValue": "true",
    "description": "Whether to flip the placement when the floating element overflows the boundary."
  },
  {
    "name": "getAnchorRect",
    "type": "( element: | HTMLElement | VirtualElement, ) => { height?: number width?: number x?: number y?: number }",
    "description": "Function that returns the anchor rect"
  },
  {
    "name": "gutter",
    "type": "number",
    "defaultValue": "8",
    "description": "The main axis offset or gap between the reference and floating element"
  },
  {
    "name": "hideWhenDetached",
    "type": "boolean",
    "description": "Whether the popover should be hidden when the reference element is detached"
  },
  {
    "name": "listeners",
    "type": "boolean | { ancestorResize?: boolean ancestorScroll?: boolean animationFrame?: boolean elementResize?: boolean layoutShift?: boolean }",
    "defaultValue": "true",
    "description": "Options to activate auto-update listeners"
  },
  {
    "name": "offset",
    "type": "{ crossAxis?: number mainAxis?: number }",
    "description": "The offset of the floating element"
  },
  {
    "name": "onComplete",
    "type": "( data: ComputePositionReturn, ) => void",
    "description": "Function called when the placement is computed"
  },
  {
    "name": "onPositioned",
    "type": "(data: { placed: boolean }) => void",
    "description": "Function called when the floating element is positioned or not"
  },
  {
    "name": "overflowPadding",
    "type": "number",
    "description": "The virtual padding around the viewport edges to check for overflow"
  },
  {
    "name": "overlap",
    "type": "boolean",
    "defaultValue": "false",
    "description": "Whether the floating element can overlap the reference element"
  },
  {
    "name": "placement",
    "type": "'bottom' | 'bottom-end' | 'bottom-start' | 'left' | 'left-end' | 'left-start' | 'right' | 'right-end' | 'right-start' | 'top' | 'top-end' | 'top-start'",
    "defaultValue": "'bottom'",
    "description": "The initial placement of the floating element"
  },
  {
    "name": "sameWidth",
    "type": "boolean",
    "description": "Whether to make the floating element same width as the reference element"
  },
  {
    "name": "shift",
    "type": "number",
    "description": "The secondary axis offset or gap between the reference and floating elements"
  },
  {
    "name": "slide",
    "type": "boolean",
    "description": "Whether the popover should slide when it overflows."
  },
  {
    "name": "strategy",
    "type": "'absolute' | 'fixed'",
    "defaultValue": "'absolute'",
    "description": "The strategy to use for positioning"
  },
  {
    "name": "updatePosition",
    "type": "(data: { updatePosition: () => Promise<void> }) => void | Promise<void>",
    "description": "A callback that will be called when the popover needs to calculate its position."
  }
]
```

### [Element API](https://react-next.qui.qualcomm.com/components/menu#element-api)

The following describes the individual subcomponents of the `Menu` component.

#### [\<Menu.Button>](https://react-next.qui.qualcomm.com/components/menu#menu-button)

```json
[
  {
    "name": "children",
    "type": "ReactNode",
    "description": "React [children](https://react.dev/learn/passing-props-to-a-component#passing-jsx-as-children) prop."
  },
  {
    "name": "density",
    "type": "'default' | 'compact'",
    "defaultValue": "'default'",
    "description": "The density of the button. Governs padding and height."
  },
  {
    "name": "disabled",
    "type": "boolean",
    "defaultValue": "false",
    "description": "Controls whether the component is interactive. When `true`, pointer/focus events are blocked, and the component is visually dimmed."
  },
  {
    "name": "emphasis",
    "type": "'neutral' | 'primary' | 'danger' | 'white-persistent' | 'black-persistent'",
    "defaultValue": "'neutral'",
    "description": "The style variant of the button. Governs colors."
  },
  {
    "name": "render",
    "type": "ReactElement | (( props: object, ) => ReactElement)",
    "description": "Allows you to replace the component's HTML element with a different tag or component. [Learn more](https://react-next.qui.qualcomm.com/polymorphic-components)"
  },
  {
    "name": "size",
    "type": "'sm' | 'md' | 'lg'",
    "defaultValue": "'md'",
    "description": "The size of the component and its icons."
  },
  {
    "name": "startIcon",
    "type": "LucideIcon | ReactNode",
    "description": "Icon positioned before the text content. If supplied as a `LucideIcon`, the size will automatically match the size prop. Supply as a `ReactElement` for additional customization."

  {
    "name": "variant",
    "type": "'fill' | 'ghost' | 'outline'",
```

```json
    "name": "onValueChange",
    "type": "( value: string, ) => void",
    "description": ""
  },
  {
    "name": "render",

  },
  {

    "type": "string",
    "description": ""
```
```json
  }
]
```

## Number Input

```tsx
import {NumberInput} from "@qualcomm-ui/react/number-input"
```

### [Examples](https://react-next.qui.qualcomm.com/components/number-input#examples)

#### [Simple](https://react-next.qui.qualcomm.com/components/number-input#simple)

The simple API bundles all subcomponents together into a single component.

```tsx
import type {ReactElement} from "react"

import {NumberInput} from "@qualcomm-ui/react/number-input"

export function NumberInputSimpleDemo(): ReactElement {
  return (
    <NumberInput className="w-72" label="Label" placeholder="Enter a number" />
  )
}

```

#### [Composite](https://react-next.qui.qualcomm.com/components/number-input#composite)

The composite pattern provides full control over the component's elements by composing individual subcomponents.

```tsx
import type {ReactElement} from "react"

import {NumberInput} from "@qualcomm-ui/react/number-input"

export function NumberInputCompositeDemo(): ReactElement {
  return (
    <NumberInput.Root className="w-72">
      <NumberInput.Label>Label</NumberInput.Label>
      <NumberInput.InputGroup>
        <NumberInput.Input placeholder="Enter a number" />
        <NumberInput.Control />
        <NumberInput.ErrorIndicator />
      </NumberInput.InputGroup>
      <NumberInput.ErrorText>Error</NumberInput.ErrorText>
    </NumberInput.Root>
  )
}

```

#### [Min and Max values](https://react-next.qui.qualcomm.com/components/number-input#min-and-max-values)

Pass the [min](https://react-next.qui.qualcomm.com/components/number-input#min) and [max](https://react-next.qui.qualcomm.com/components/number-input#max) props to the root component to set the minimum and maximum values of the number input.

If the value entered is less than min or greater than max, it will be clamped to the nearest boundary on blur or enter.

```tsx
import type {ReactElement} from "react"

import {NumberInput} from "@qualcomm-ui/react/number-input"

export function NumberInputMinMaxDemo(): ReactElement {
  return (
    <NumberInput className="w-72" defaultValue="5" max={10} min={5} />
  )
}

```

#### [Step interval](https://react-next.qui.qualcomm.com/components/number-input#step-interval)

Pass the [step](https://react-next.qui.qualcomm.com/components/number-input#step) prop to the root component to set the increment or decrement step interval.

```tsx
import type {ReactElement} from "react"

import {NumberInput} from "@qualcomm-ui/react/number-input"

export function NumberInputStepDemo(): ReactElement {
  return (
    <NumberInput className="w-72" placeholder="Enter a number" step={3} />
  )
}

```

#### [States](https://react-next.qui.qualcomm.com/components/number-input#states)

The following shows how the TextInput component appears in each interactive state.

```tsx
import type {ReactElement} from "react"

import {NumberInput} from "@qualcomm-ui/react/number-input"

export function NumberInputStatesDemo(): ReactElement {
  return (
    <div className="flex w-60 flex-col gap-4">
      <NumberInput disabled label="Disabled" placeholder="Disabled" />
      <NumberInput label="Read only" placeholder="Read only" readOnly />
      <NumberInput label="Required" placeholder="Required" required />
      <NumberInput
        errorText="Invalid"
        invalid
        label="Invalid"
        placeholder="Invalid"
      />
    </div>
  )
}

```

#### [Controlled Value](https://react-next.qui.qualcomm.com/components/number-input#controlled-value)

Use the [value](https://react-next.qui.qualcomm.com/components/number-input#value), [onValueChange](https://react-next.qui.qualcomm.com/components/number-input#onValueChange) and [defaultValue](https://react-next.qui.qualcomm.com/components/number-input#defaultValue) props to control the value of the input field. These props follow our [controlled state](/patterns/controlled-state) pattern.

```tsx
import {type ReactElement, useState} from "react"

import {NumberInput} from "@qualcomm-ui/react/number-input"

export function NumberInputControlledDemo(): ReactElement {
  const [value, setValue] = useState<string>("")
  return (
    <NumberInput
      className="w-72"
      label="Controlled Value"
      onValueChange={({value}) => setValue(value)}
      placeholder="Placeholder"
      value={value}
    />
  )
}

```

#### [Sizes](https://react-next.qui.qualcomm.com/components/number-input#sizes)

Customize size using the [size](https://react-next.qui.qualcomm.com/components/number-input#size) prop. The default size is `md`.

```tsx
import type {ReactElement} from "react"

import {Sigma} from "lucide-react"

import {NumberInput} from "@qualcomm-ui/react/number-input"

export function NumberInputSizesDemo(): ReactElement {
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col items-start gap-4">
      <NumberInput
        className="w-56"
        placeholder="sm"
        size="sm"
        startIcon={Sigma}
      />
      <NumberInput className="w-64" placeholder="md" startIcon={Sigma} />
      <NumberInput
        className="w-72"
        placeholder="lg"
        size="lg"
        startIcon={Sigma}
      />
    </div>
  )
}

```

#### [Error Text and Indicator](https://react-next.qui.qualcomm.com/components/number-input#error-text-and-indicator)

Error messages are displayed using two props:

* [invalid](https://react-next.qui.qualcomm.com/components/number-input#invalid)
* [errorText](https://react-next.qui.qualcomm.com/components/number-input#errorText) (or the `NumberInput.ErrorText` component when using the composite API)

The error text and indicator will only render when [invalid](https://react-next.qui.qualcomm.com/components/number-input#invalid) is true.

```tsx
import {type ReactElement, useState} from "react"

import {NumberInput} from "@qualcomm-ui/react/number-input"

export function NumberInputErrorTextDemo(): ReactElement {
  const [value, setValue] = useState<string>("")

  return (
    <NumberInput
      className="w-72"
      errorText="You must enter a value"
      invalid={!value}
      label="Label"
      onValueChange={({value}) => setValue(value)}
      placeholder="Enter a value"
      value={value}
    />
  )
}

```

## Pagination

Enables the user to select a specific page from a range of pages.

```tsx
import {Pagination} from "@qualcomm-ui/react/pagination"
```

### [Examples](https://react-next.qui.qualcomm.com/components/pagination#examples)

#### [Showcase](https://react-next.qui.qualcomm.com/components/pagination#showcase)

The component renders the number of pages based on the `count` and `pageSize` props.

```tsx
import type {ReactElement} from "react"

import {Pagination} from "@qualcomm-ui/react/pagination"

export function PaginationShowcaseDemo(): ReactElement {
  return (
    <Pagination.Root count={120} pageSize={10}>
      <Pagination.PageButtons />
    </Pagination.Root>
  )
}

```

#### [Sizes](https://react-next.qui.qualcomm.com/components/pagination#sizes)

Customize the size of the component and its elements using the [size](https://react-next.qui.qualcomm.com/components/pagination#size) prop.

```tsx
import type {ReactElement} from "react"

import {Pagination} from "@qualcomm-ui/react/pagination"

export function PaginationSizesDemo(): ReactElement {
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
      <Pagination.Root count={120} defaultPageSize={10} size="sm">
        <Pagination.PageButtons />
        <Pagination.PageSize options={[10, 20, 50, 100]}>
          <Pagination.PageSizeLabel>Page size</Pagination.PageSizeLabel>
        </Pagination.PageSize>
      </Pagination.Root>

      <Pagination.Root count={120} defaultPageSize={10} size="md">
        <Pagination.PageButtons />
        <Pagination.PageSize options={[10, 20, 50, 100]}>
          <Pagination.PageSizeLabel>Page size</Pagination.PageSizeLabel>
        </Pagination.PageSize>
      </Pagination.Root>
    </div>
  )
}

```

#### [Page Size](https://react-next.qui.qualcomm.com/components/pagination#page-size)

Render a dropdown via `<Pagination.PageSize>` to adjust the number of data items per page.

```tsx
import type {ReactElement} from "react"

import {Pagination} from "@qualcomm-ui/react/pagination"

export function PaginationPageSizeDemo(): ReactElement {
  return (
    <Pagination.Root count={120} defaultPageSize={10}>
      <Pagination.PageButtons />
      <Pagination.PageSize options={[10, 20, 50, 100]}>
        <Pagination.PageSizeLabel>Page size</Pagination.PageSizeLabel>
      </Pagination.PageSize>
    </Pagination.Root>
  )
}

```

#### [Page Metadata](https://react-next.qui.qualcomm.com/components/pagination#page-metadata)

Show metadata about the current and total pages with `<Pagination.PageMetadata>`

To customize the text, supply children as a render prop.

```tsx
import type {ReactElement} from "react"

import {Pagination} from "@qualcomm-ui/react/pagination"

export function PaginationMetadataDemo(): ReactElement {
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col">
      <div className="text-neutral-primary font-heading-xxs mb-2">Default</div>
      <Pagination.Root count={115} pageSize={10}>
        <Pagination.PageMetadata />
        <Pagination.PageButtons />
      </Pagination.Root>

      <div className="text-neutral-primary font-heading-xxs mt-4 mb-2">
        Customized
      </div>
      <Pagination.Root count={115} pageSize={10}>
        <Pagination.PageMetadata>
          {({count, page, pageCount, pageEnd, pageStart}) => (
            <div className="flex gap-2">
              <span>
                {pageStart}-{pageEnd} of {count}
              </span>
              <span>
                (page {page} of {pageCount})
              </span>
            </div>
          )}
        </Pagination.PageMetadata>
        <Pagination.PageButtons />
      </Pagination.Root>
    </div>
  )
}

```

#### [Ranges](https://react-next.qui.qualcomm.com/components/pagination#ranges)

```tsx
import type {ReactElement} from "react"

import {Pagination} from "@qualcomm-ui/react/pagination"

export function PaginationRangesDemo(): ReactElement {
  return (
    <div className="grid justify-center gap-4">
      <Pagination.Root count={12} defaultPage={6} siblingCount={0}>
        <Pagination.PageButtons />
      </Pagination.Root>

      {/* Default */}
      <Pagination.Root count={12} defaultPage={6}>
        <Pagination.PageButtons />
      </Pagination.Root>

      <Pagination.Root count={12} defaultPage={6} siblingCount={2}>
        <Pagination.PageButtons />
      </Pagination.Root>
    </div>
  )
}

```

#### [Controlled State](https://react-next.qui.qualcomm.com/components/pagination#controlled-state)

The Pagination component follows our [controlled state](/patterns/controlled-state) pattern for its [page](https://react-next.qui.qualcomm.com/components/pagination#page) and [pageSize](https://react-next.qui.qualcomm.com/components/pagination#pageSize) props.

By default, the component is uncontrolled and manages these values internally. You may optionally control the current page or page size by supplying the properties and their corresponding onChange functions:

```tsx
import {type ReactElement, useState} from "react"

import {Pagination} from "@qualcomm-ui/react/pagination"

export function PaginationControlledStateDemo(): ReactElement {
  const [page, setPage] = useState(1)
  const [pageSize, setPageSize] = useState(10)

  return (
    <Pagination.Root
      count={100}
      onPageChange={(pageValue) => setPage(pageValue)}
      onPageSizeChange={(pageSizeValue) => {
        setPageSize(pageSizeValue)
      }}
      page={page}
      pageSize={pageSize}
    >
      <Pagination.PageSize options={[5, 10, 25, 50]}>
        <Pagination.PageSizeLabel>Items per page:</Pagination.PageSizeLabel>
      </Pagination.PageSize>

      <Pagination.PageButtons />

      <Pagination.PageMetadata>
        {({count, pageEnd, pageStart}) => (
          <span>
            Showing {pageStart}-{pageEnd} of {count} items
          </span>
        )}
      </Pagination.PageMetadata>
    </Pagination.Root>
  )
}

```

### [Shortcuts](https://react-next.qui.qualcomm.com/components/pagination#shortcuts)

Shortcuts enable quick implementations for common use cases.

#### [PageItems](https://react-next.qui.qualcomm.com/components/pagination#page-items)

Rendering `<Pagination.PageItems />` is shorthand for this:

```tsx
<PaginationContext>
  {(context) => (
    <>
      {context.pageItems.map((item, index) => {
        const itemProps = context.getPageItemProps(item)
        return <Pagination.PageItem key={index} {...itemProps} />
      })}
      {children}
    </>
  )}
</PaginationContext>
```

#### [PageSize](https://react-next.qui.qualcomm.com/components/pagination#page-size-1)

The `<Pagination.PageSize />` shortcut is a convenient dropdown for selecting the number of items per page. It's shorthand for this:

```tsx
<PolymorphicElement as="div" {...mergedProps}>
  {children}
  <Menu.Root
    onSelect={(value) => {
      setPageSize(parseInt(value))
    }}
    size={qdsContext.size}
  >
    <Menu.Trigger>
      <Menu.Button
        aria-labelledby={pageSizeLabelId}
        emphasis="neutral"
        size={qdsContext.size}
        variant="outline"
      >
        {pageSize}
      </Menu.Button>
    </Menu.Trigger>
    <Portal>
      <Menu.Positioner>
        <Menu.Content>
          {options.map((opt) => (
            <Menu.Item key={opt} value={`${opt}`}>
              {opt}
            </Menu.Item>
          ))}
        </Menu.Content>
      </Menu.Positioner>
    </Portal>
  </Menu.Root>
</PolymorphicElement>
```

#### [PageButtons](https://react-next.qui.qualcomm.com/components/pagination#page-buttons)

The `<Pagination.PageButtons>` shortcut renders the prev, next, and individual page buttons:

```tsx
<ActionGroup {...props}>
  <Pagination.PrevTrigger />
  <Pagination.PageItems />
  <Pagination.NextTrigger />
</ActionGroup>
```

### [API](https://react-next.qui.qualcomm.com/components/pagination#api)

#### [PageMetadata](https://react-next.qui.qualcomm.com/components/pagination#page-metadata-1)

Metadata about the current page is available as a render prop from the `<Pagination.PageMetadata>` element:

```tsx
<Pagination.PageMetadata>
  {({count, page, pageCount, pageEnd, pageStart}) => {
    // ...
  }}
</Pagination.PageMetadata>
```
